-

⇒ Duration

unsignedInt(1:)

W

[MANDATORY] Time between the start of the state change and the return to its original state. Duration is measured in milliseconds.

-

2.20

TransitionLow()

command

-

Generate a Pulse with a FallingEdge transition for a duration of Duration.

-

2.20

⇒ Input.

arguments

-

Input arguments.

-

⇒ Duration

unsignedInt(1:)

W

[MANDATORY] Time between the start of the state change and the return to its original state. Duration is measured in milliseconds.

2.20

Device.IoTCapability.{i}.Binding.{i}.

object(0:)

W

This Binding only exists in a Sensor IoTCapability. Adding a Binding instance to a Control IoTCapability MUST fail. Each entry in this table describes the link between this Sensor IoTCapability and a specific Control IoTCapability.

When a Sensor IoT Capability is bound to a Control IoT Capability, then for each Value parameter updates of the Sensor IoTCapability, the Agent evaluates the needs to update the Value parameter of the bound Control IoTCapability.

At most one entry in this table can exist with a given value for Alias. On creation of a new table entry, the Agent MUST (if not supplied by the Controller on creation) choose an initial value for Alias such that the new entry does not conflict with any existing entries.

-

2.20

Alias

string(:64)

WO

[Alias] A non-volatile unique key used to reference this instance. Alias provides a mechanism for a Controller to label this instance for future reference.

The following mandatory constraints MUST be enforced:

  • The value MUST NOT be empty.

  • The value MUST start with a letter.

  • If the value is not assigned by the Controller at creation time, the Agent MUST assign a value with an "cpe-" prefix.

Once it's been set, this parameter is immutable.

If the value isn't assigned by the Controller on creation, the Agent MUST choose an initial value that doesn't conflict with any existing entries.

This is a non-functional key and its value MUST NOT change once it's been assigned by the Controller or set internally by the Agent.

-

2.20

FriendlyName

string(:64)

W

Friendly name for this binding.

-

2.20

Description

string(:256)

W

Description of the binding's intent and function.

-

2.20

Enable

boolean

W

When true, the binding is active; otherwise, no data flow occurs and the Value parameter of ControlIoTCapability is not updated.

-

2.20

ControlIoTCapability

string

W

The value MUST be the Path Name of a row in the IoTCapability. table. If the referenced object is deleted, the parameter value MUST be set to an empty string. A valid ControlIoTCapability is a reference to a Control IoTCapability in the IoTCapability table. Setting ControlIoTCapability to a Sensor IoTCapability MUST fail. It references the Control IoTCapability to use as the binding destination on which the Agent updates the Value parameter. As a mountable object IoTCapability may also be mounted on one of the following mountpoints:

  • Node.{i}.,
  • ProxiedDevice.{i}.,
  • ProxiedDevice.{i}.Node.{i}.

-

2.20

Device.IoTCapability.{i}.PulseSensor.

object(0:1)

R

A PulseSensor is used to reflect the functionality of a sensor that changed state from low to high or high to low. A PulseSensor provides a trigger on the rising edge of this state change, which is identified by Transition. Some PulseSensor instances have the possibility to provide a pulse duration Duration together with the latest state change.

This object MUST be present if, and only if, Class is PulseSensor.

This object is a member of a union, i.e., it is a member of a group of objects of which only one can exist at a given time.

-

2.20

Type

string

R

[IoTPulseType] Describes the type of pulse that the PulseSensor instance is representing. Enumeration of:

  • PushButton
  • MagneticContact
  • ElectronicLock
  • MotionTrigger

-

2.20

Description

string(:256)

R

Human-readable description of the PulseSensor. For example, a more detailed description of Type.

-

2.20

LastChange

dateTime

R

The date and time in UTC when Duration or Transitionhas been changed to the current value.

-

2.20

LastContactTime

dateTime

R

The date and time in UTC when the PulseSensor has received data.

-

2.20

Duration

unsignedInt

R

Some PulseSensor instances can provide a duration together with the latest state change. The duration is an indication of the time, measured in milliseconds, between the start of the state change and the end of the state change: i.e., returning to its orignal state. The original state can be a low state or high state.

-

2.20

Unit

unsignedInt

R

Unit in which Duration is expressed in milliseconds.

-

2.20

Transition

string

R

Indicates the moment a pulse is generated. This can be a RisingEdge transition: in case the sensor changes from low to high state. FallingEdge transition: in case the sensor changes from high to low state.

Enumeration of:

  • RisingEdge (The sensor changes from low to high state)
  • FallingEdge (The sensor changes from high to low state)
